    <script>
    async function auditControl(controlId, assessmentId, result, btn, comments) {
      const payload = {};
      if (result) {
        payload.result = result;
        // Deactivate siblings, activate this
        if (btn) {
          btn.closest('.audit-row').querySelectorAll('.audit-btn').forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
          btn.classList.add('active');
        }
      }
      const commentEl = document.getElementById('audit-comment-' + controlId);
      payload.comments = comments || (commentEl ? commentEl.value : '');
      if (result) payload.result = result;
    
      try {
        await fetch('/admin/assessments/' + assessmentId + '/audit-control/' + controlId, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify(payload)
        });
      } catch(e) { console.error(e); }
    }
    
    async function loadComments(controlId) {
      try {
        const res = await fetch('/api/comments/' + controlId);
        const data = await res.json();
        const thread = document.getElementById('thread-' + controlId);
        if (data.comments && data.comments.length) {
          thread.innerHTML = data.comments.map(c =>
            '<div class="comment-item">' +
            '<span class="comment-author">' + c.user_name + '</span> ' +
            '<span class="comment-time">' + new Date(c.created_at).toLocaleString() + '</span>' +
            '<div>' + c.content + '</div></div>'
          ).join('');
        } else {
          thread.innerHTML = '<div class="text-muted small text-center py-2">No comments yet</div>';
        }
      } catch(e) { console.error(e); }
    }
    
    async function addComment(controlId, assessmentId) {
      const input = document.getElementById('commentInput-' + controlId);
      if (!input.value.trim()) return;
      try {
        await fetch('/api/comments/' + controlId, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ content: input.value, user_name: 'E2E Assessor', user_role: 'assessor' })
        });
        input.value = '';
        loadComments(controlId);
      } catch(e) { console.error(e); }
    }
    
    // Load comments when expanded
    document.querySelectorAll('[id^="comments-"]').forEach(el => {
      el.addEventListener('show.bs.collapse', () => {
        const controlId = el.id.replace('comments-', '');
        loadComments(controlId);
      });
    });
    </script>
    
    
    <script>
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    // AI EVIDENCE NARRATIVE GENERATION
    // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
    const PROJECT_CONTEXT = {"name":"E2E Tailoring Project","description":"A protected B Azure case management system with APIs, audit logging, identity controls, and supporting documentation.","technologies":"[\"azure\"]","hosting_type":"azure","confidentiality_level":"protected-b","integrity_level":"medium","availability_level":"medium","security_profile":"PBMM","security_framework":"ITSG-33","framework_summary":"ITSG-33 profile PBMM; C/I/A protected-b / medium / medium"};
    const ASSESSMENT_ID = '2';
    
    document.querySelectorAll('.assignment-mode').forEach(select => {
      const sync = () => {
        const scope = select.dataset.scope;
        document.querySelectorAll('.assignment-existing-' + scope).forEach(el => el.classList.toggle('d-none', select.value !== 'existing'));
        document.querySelectorAll('.assignment-invite-' + scope).forEach(el => el.classList.toggle('d-none', select.value !== 'invite'));
      };
      select.addEventListener('change', sync);
      sync();
    });
    
    async function aiGenerateFromDocuments() {
      const statusEl = document.getElementById('aiDocGuidanceStatus');
      const previewEl = document.getElementById('aiDocGuidancePreview');
      const documentIds = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.ai-doc-checkbox:checked')).map(cb => cb.value);
      const scope = document.getElementById('aiControlScope')?.value || 'visible';
      const controlIds = scope === 'selected'
        ? Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.ai-control-checkbox:checked')).map(cb => cb.value)
        : Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[id^="ai-ev-btn-"]')).map(btn => btn.id.replace('ai-ev-btn-', ''));
    
      if (!documentIds.length) {
        statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-danger">Select at least one document.</span>';
        return;
      }
      if (!controlIds.length) {
        statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-danger">Select at least one control.</span>';
        return;
      }
    
      statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-1"></span>Analyzing selected documentation...';
      previewEl.innerHTML = '';
    
      try {
        const resp = await fetch('/admin/assessments/' + ASSESSMENT_ID + '/ai/document-guidance', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ document_ids: documentIds, control_db_ids: controlIds.slice(0, 10) })
        });
        const data = await resp.json();
        if (!data.success) throw new Error(data.error || 'AI guidance failed');
        window._documentGuidancePreview = data.guidance;
        statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-success"><i class="bi bi-check-circle me-1"></i>Preview ready. Review before saving.</span>';
        previewEl.innerHTML = data.guidance.map((item, index) => `
          <div class="border rounded p-3 mb-2 ai-doc-guidance-card" data-index="${index}">
            <div class="d-flex justify-content-between gap-2">
              <div>
                <strong>${item.controlId}</strong> ${item.title}
                ${item.currentGuidance ? '<span class="badge bg-warning text-dark ms-2">Existing guidance</span>' : ''}
              </div>
              <div class="form-check">
                <input class="form-check-input ai-save-guidance" type="checkbox" id="ai-save-${index}" checked>
                <label class="form-check-label small" for="ai-save-${index}">Save</label>
              </div>
            </div>
            <textarea class="form-control form-control-sm mt-2 ai-guidance-text" rows="5">${item.guidance}</textarea>
            ${item.currentGuidance ? `<details class="small mt-2"><summary>Existing guidance</summary><div class="text-muted mt-1">${item.currentGuidance}</div></details>` : ''}
          </div>
        `).join('') + `
          <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-success" onclick="aiSaveDocumentGuidance()">
            <i class="bi bi-save me-1"></i>Save Approved Guidance
          </button>`;
      } catch (err) {
        statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-danger">' + err.message + '</span>';
      }
    }
    
    async function aiSaveDocumentGuidance() {
      const cards = Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('.ai-doc-guidance-card'));
      const items = cards.map(card => {
        const index = Number(card.dataset.index);
        const item = window._documentGuidancePreview[index];
        return {
          controlDbId: item.controlDbId,
          guidance: card.querySelector('.ai-guidance-text').value,
          confirmOverwrite: true,
          save: card.querySelector('.ai-save-guidance').checked
        };
      }).filter(item => item.save);
      const statusEl = document.getElementById('aiDocGuidanceStatus');
      statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-1"></span>Saving approved guidance...';
      const resp = await fetch('/admin/assessments/' + ASSESSMENT_ID + '/ai/document-guidance/save', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ items })
      });
      const data = await resp.json();
      if (data.success) {
        statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-success"><i class="bi bi-check-circle me-1"></i>Saved ' + data.saved + ' guidance item(s). Refreshing...</span>';
        setTimeout(() => window.location.reload(), 700);
      } else {
        statusEl.innerHTML = '<span class="text-danger">' + (data.error || 'Save failed') + '</span>';
      }
    }
    
    // ── Single control evidence GUIDANCE generation (assessor generates for client) ──
    async function aiGenerateGuidance(controlDbId, controlId, title) {
      const btn = document.getElementById('ai-ev-btn-' + controlDbId);
      const statusEl = document.getElementById('ai-ev-status-' + controlDbId);
      const resultEl = document.getElementById('ai-ev-result-' + controlDbId);
    
      btn.disabled = true;
      btn.innerHTML = '<span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-1"></span>Generating...';
      statusEl.style.display = 'inline';
      statusEl.textContent = 'AI is drafting evidence guidance for the client...';
    
      try {
        const resp = await fetch('/api/ai/evidence-guidance', {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({
            controlId, controlTitle: title,
            controlDescription: title,
            projectContext: PROJECT_CONTEXT
          })
        });
        const data = await resp.json();
        if (!data.success) throw new Error(data.error || 'Failed');
    
        resultEl.style.display = 'block';
        resultEl.innerHTML = `
          <div style="background:#f5f3ff; border:1px solid #e9d5ff; border-radius:8px; padding:12px;">
            <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-2">
              <small class="fw-semibold" style="color:#6d28d9;"><i class="bi bi-stars me-1"></i>AI-Generated Evidence Guidance</small>
              <div class="d-flex gap-1">
                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-success" style="font-size:.75rem;" onclick="aiSaveGuidance(${controlDbId})">
                  <i class="bi bi-check-lg me-1"></i>Save to Control
                </button>
                <button class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-secondary" style="font-size:.75rem;" onclick="aiCopyNarrative(this, ${controlDbId})">
                  <i class="bi bi-clipboard me-1"></i>Copy
                </button>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div class="small" style="white-space:pre-wrap; line-height:1.6;" id="ai-narrative-text-${controlDbId}">${data.guidance}</div>
            <small class="text-muted d-block mt-2"><i class="bi bi-info-circle me-1"></i>This guidance will be shown to the client in their evidence submission portal.</small>
          </div>`;
    
        statusEl.textContent = 'Guidance ready — click "Save to Control" to attach it.';
        statusEl.style.color = '#16a34a';
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-stars me-1"></i>Regenerate Guidance';
        btn.disabled = false;
      } catch (err) {
        statusEl.textContent = 'Error: ' + err.message;
        statusEl.style.color = '#dc3545';
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-stars me-1"></i>Retry';
        btn.disabled = false;
      }
    }
    
    // ── Save AI guidance to the control's evidence_guidance field ──
    async function aiSaveGuidance(controlDbId) {
      const text = document.getElementById('ai-narrative-text-' + controlDbId).textContent;
      try {
        const resp = await fetch('/api/ai/save-guidance/' + controlDbId, {
          method: 'POST',
          headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ guidance: text })
        });
        const data = await resp.json();
        if (data.success) {
          const statusEl = document.getElementById('ai-ev-status-' + controlDbId);
          statusEl.textContent = '✓ Guidance saved — client will see it in their portal.';
          statusEl.style.color = '#16a34a';
          // Update the guidance display on page if it exists
          const card = document.getElementById('control-' + controlDbId) || document.getElementById('ai-ev-btn-' + controlDbId)?.closest('.control-card');
          if (card) {
            let guidanceEl = card.querySelector('[style*="fff8e1"]');
            if (guidanceEl) {
              guidanceEl.querySelector('small:last-child').textContent = text;
            } else {
              const newEl = document.createElement('div');
              newEl.className = 'mt-2 p-2 rounded';
              newEl.style.background = '#fff8e1';
              newEl.innerHTML = '<small class="text-muted fw-semibold d-block mb-1"><i class="bi bi-lightbulb me-1"></i>Evidence Guidance</small><small>' + text + '</small>';
              const aiSection = document.getElementById('ai-ev-result-' + controlDbId);
              if (aiSection) aiSection.parentNode.insertBefore(newEl, aiSection);
            }
          }
        }
      } catch(e) { console.error(e); }
    }
    
    // ── Copy narrative to clipboard ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    function aiCopyNarrative(btn, controlDbId) {
      const text = document.getElementById('ai-narrative-text-' + controlDbId).textContent;
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(text).then(() => {
        btn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-check2 me-1"></i>Copied!';
        setTimeout(() => { btn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-clipboard me-1"></i>Copy'; }, 2000);
      });
    }
    
    // Page setup (Bulk Evidence Guidance removed — superseded by the Aegis SA Assistant)
    document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', function() {
      // ── Family Nav: scroll spy + filter ──
      const familySections = document.querySelectorAll('[id^="family-"]');
      const navItems = document.querySelectorAll('.family-nav-item');
      const filterInput = document.getElementById('familyFilter');
    
      // Scroll spy
      if (familySections.length && navItems.length) {
        const observer = new IntersectionObserver(entries => {
          entries.forEach(entry => {
            if (entry.isIntersecting) {
              const id = entry.target.id;
              navItems.forEach(item => {
                item.classList.toggle('active', item.getAttribute('href') === '#' + id);
              });
            }
          });
        }, { rootMargin: '-80px 0px -70% 0px', threshold: 0 });
    
        familySections.forEach(sec => observer.observe(sec));
    
        // Smooth scroll. The sticky-header offset is handled purely in CSS via
        // `scroll-margin-top` on the family sections, so scrollIntoView lands on the
        // clicked family's first control every time (no racy post-scroll nudge).
        navItems.forEach(item => {
          item.addEventListener('click', function(e) {
            e.preventDefault();
            const target = document.querySelector(this.getAttribute('href'));
            if (target) target.scrollIntoView({ behavior: 'smooth', block: 'start' });
          });
        });
      }
    
      // Family filter
      if (filterInput) {
        filterInput.addEventListener('input', function() {
          const q = this.value.toLowerCase();
          navItems.forEach(item => {
            const code = item.dataset.family || '';
            const name = item.querySelector('.family-nav-name')?.textContent || '';
            item.style.display = (code.toLowerCase().includes(q) || name.toLowerCase().includes(q)) ? '' : 'none';
          });
        });
      }
    });
    </script>
    
    <style>
    /* Anchor offset so clicking a family lands on its first control, clear of the
       sticky page header (rather than scrolling under it onto the previous family). */
    [id^="family-"] { scroll-margin-top: 90px; }
    /* Family Navigation Sidebar */
    .family-nav {
      position: sticky;
      top: 80px;
      max-height: calc(100vh - 100px);
      overflow-y: auto;
      background: white;
      border: 1px solid #dee2e6;
      border-radius: 8px;
      padding: 12px;
      font-size: .82rem;
    }
    .family-nav::-webkit-scrollbar { width: 4px; }
    .family-nav::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb { background: #ccc; border-radius: 2px; }
    .family-nav-header {
      font-weight: 700;
      font-size: .85rem;
      color: var(--gc-nav, #333);
      padding-bottom: 8px;
      border-bottom: 2px solid var(--gc-red, #af3c43);
      margin-bottom: 8px;
    }
    .family-nav-list {
      max-height: calc(100vh - 280px);
      overflow-y: auto;
    }
    .family-nav-item {
      display: flex;
      align-items: center;
      gap: 6px;
      padding: 5px 8px;
      border-radius: 4px;
      text-decoration: none;
      color: #333;
      font-size: .78rem;
      transition: all .15s;
      border-left: 3px solid transparent;
    }
    .family-nav-item:hover {
      background: #f0f4f8;
      color: var(--gc-link, #2b4380);
    }
    .family-nav-item.active {
      background: #e8f0fe;
      border-left-color: var(--gc-link, #2b4380);
      font-weight: 600;
      color: var(--gc-link, #2b4380);
    }
    .family-nav-code {
      font-family: monospace;
      font-weight: 700;
      font-size: .75rem;
      min-width: 28px;
      color: #555;
    }
    .family-nav-name {
      flex: 1;
      overflow: hidden;
      text-overflow: ellipsis;
      white-space: nowrap;
    }
    .family-nav-count {
      font-size: .7rem;
      background: #e9ecef;
      border-radius: 10px;
      padding: 1px 6px;
      color: #666;
      min-width: 20px;
      text-align: center;
    }
    .family-nav-item.active .family-nav-count {
      background: var(--gc-link, #2b4380);
      color: white;
    }
    .family-nav-footer {
      border-top: 1px solid #dee2e6;
      padding-top: 8px;
    }
    /* Mobile: show as horizontal strip */
    @media (max-width: 991.98px) {
      .family-nav-mobile {
        display: flex;
        overflow-x: auto;
        gap: 4px;
        padding: 8px 0;
        margin-bottom: 16px;
        border-bottom: 1px solid #dee2e6;
      }
      .family-nav-mobile .family-nav-item {
        white-space: nowrap;
        flex-shrink: 0;
        border-left: none;
        border-bottom: 2px solid transparent;
      }
      .family-nav-mobile .family-nav-item.active {
        border-bottom-color: var(--gc-link, #2b4380);
        border-left: none;
      }
    }
    </style>
    
    <div class="modal fade" id="versionHistoryModal" tabindex="-1" aria-hidden="true">
      <div class="modal-dialog modal-lg modal-dialog-scrollable">
        <div class="modal-content">
          <div class="modal-header">
            <h5 class="modal-title"><i class="bi bi-clock-history me-2"></i>Version history</h5>
            <button type="button" class="btn-close" data-bs-dismiss="modal" aria-label="Close"></button>
          </div>
          <div class="modal-body">
            <div class="d-flex justify-content-between align-items-center mb-3">
              <span class="text-muted small">Every change checkpoint is kept for audit. Reverting to an earlier
                version creates a new active version — nothing is lost.</span>
              <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary ms-2 flex-shrink-0" onclick="saaCreateCheckpoint()">
                <i class="bi bi-bookmark-plus me-1"></i>Create checkpoint
              </button>
            </div>
            <div class="list-group">
              <div class="list-group-item d-flex justify-content-between align-items-start gap-3">
                <div>
                  <div class="d-flex align-items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                    <span class="badge bg-secondary">v1</span>
                    <strong>Created</strong>
                    <span class="badge bg-success">Active</span>
                  </div>
                  <div class="small text-muted">Baseline — 1 control(s)</div>
                  <div class="small text-muted">
                    <i class="bi bi-person me-1"></i>E2E Assessor
                    · Aug 22, 2026, 08:59 a.m.
                  </div>
                </div>
                <div class="flex-shrink-0 d-flex flex-column align-items-end gap-1">
                  <button type="button" class="btn btn-sm btn-outline-primary"
                    onclick="saaShowVersionSummary(1)">
                    <i class="bi bi-list-check me-1"></i>Summary
                  </button>
                    <span class="text-muted small"><i class="bi bi-check2-circle me-1"></i>Current</span>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    
    <div class="modal fade" id="revertConfirmModal" tabindex="-1" aria-hidden="true" data-bs-backdrop="static">
      <div class="modal-dialog">
        <div class="modal-content border-danger">
          <div class="modal-header bg-danger text-white">
            <h5 class="modal-title"><i class="bi bi-exclamation-triangle-fill me-2"></i>Revert this assessment?</h5>
            <button type="button" class="btn-close btn-close-white" data-bs-dismiss="modal" aria-label="Close"></button>
          </div>
          <div class="modal-body">
            <p class="mb-2">You are about to revert this assessment to
              <strong>version <span id="revertVersionNum"></span></strong>
              <span id="revertVersionLabel" class="text-muted"></span>.</p>
            <div class="alert alert-warning mb-2">
              <i class="bi bi-info-circle-fill me-1"></i>
              Reverting <strong>creates a new version</strong> from that snapshot and makes it the active state.
              The <strong>current active version will no longer be the active one</strong> — it is kept in the
              version history for audit, but the assessment's live control set will be replaced by the selected
              version. Any edits made since the last checkpoint will be superseded.
            </div>
            <p class="small text-muted mb-0">Signatures and the invite code are not affected — only the tailored
              control set and security profile are restored.</p>
          </div>
          <div class="modal-footer">
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline-secondary" data-bs-dismiss="modal">Cancel</button>
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-danger" id="revertConfirmBtn">
              <i class="bi bi-arrow-counterclockwise me-1"></i>Revert &amp; create new version
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    
    <div class="modal fade" id="versionSummaryModal" tabindex="-1" aria-hidden="true">
      <div class="modal-dialog modal-lg modal-dialog-scrollable">
        <div class="modal-content">
          <div class="modal-header">
            <h5 class="modal-title"><i class="bi bi-list-check me-2"></i>Version <span id="vsVersion"></span> — summary of changes</h5>
            <button type="button" class="btn-close" data-bs-dismiss="modal" aria-label="Close"></button>
          </div>
          <div class="modal-body" id="vsBody">
            <div class="text-center text-muted py-4"><span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-2"></span>Loading…</div>
          </div>
          <div class="modal-footer">
            <a href="#" id="vsOpenFull" class="btn btn-outline-primary" target="_blank" rel="noopener">
              <i class="bi bi-box-arrow-up-right me-1"></i>Open full version in a new tab
            </a>
            <button type="button" class="btn btn-outline-secondary" data-bs-dismiss="modal">Close</button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
    
    <script>
    // Version history — revert + checkpoint + change summary.
    (function(){
      var assessmentId = 2;
      var pendingRevert = null;
    
      function esc(s){ return String(s == null ? '' : s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function(m){ return ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'})[m]; }); }
      function chips(ids, cls){ return ids.map(function(i){ return '<span class="badge ' + cls + ' me-1 mb-1">' + esc(i) + '</span>'; }).join(''); }
    
      window.saaShowVersionSummary = function(version){
        document.getElementById('vsVersion').textContent = version;
        var full = '/admin/assessments/' + assessmentId + '?version=' + version;
        document.getElementById('vsOpenFull').setAttribute('href', full);
        var body = document.getElementById('vsBody');
        body.innerHTML = '<div class="text-center text-muted py-4"><span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-2"></span>Loading…</div>';
        var hist = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(document.getElementById('versionHistoryModal'));
        if (hist) hist.hide();
        new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('versionSummaryModal')).show();
        fetch('/admin/assessments/' + assessmentId + '/versions/' + version + '/summary')
          .then(function(r){ return r.json(); })
          .then(function(d){
            if (!d.success) throw new Error(d.error || 'Could not load summary');
            var h = '';
            h += '<p class="mb-2"><strong>' + esc(d.label || ('Version ' + d.version)) + '</strong>' +
                 '<span class="text-muted"> · by ' + esc(d.author) + '</span></p>';
            if (d.summary) h += '<p class="small text-muted">' + esc(d.summary) + '</p>';
            h += '<p class="small text-muted mb-3">This version holds <strong>' + d.controlCount + '</strong> control(s)' +
                 (d.previousVersion != null ? ', compared against version ' + d.previousVersion + '.' : '.') + '</p>';
            if (d.isBaseline) {
              h += '<div class="alert alert-info mb-0"><i class="bi bi-info-circle me-1"></i>This is the baseline version — the starting point for the change history.</div>';
            } else if (!d.added.length && !d.removed.length && !d.changed.length && !d.profileChanges.length) {
              h += '<div class="alert alert-secondary mb-0">No control or profile differences from the previous version.</div>';
            } else {
              if (d.added.length)   h += '<div class="mb-3"><div class="fw-semibold text-success mb-1"><i class="bi bi-plus-circle me-1"></i>Added (' + d.added.length + ')</div>' + chips(d.added, 'bg-success-subtle text-success-emphasis border border-success-subtle') + '</div>';
              if (d.removed.length) h += '<div class="mb-3"><div class="fw-semibold text-danger mb-1"><i class="bi bi-dash-circle me-1"></i>Removed (' + d.removed.length + ')</div>' + chips(d.removed, 'bg-danger-subtle text-danger-emphasis border border-danger-subtle') + '</div>';
              if (d.changed.length) {
                h += '<div class="mb-3"><div class="fw-semibold text-primary mb-1"><i class="bi bi-pencil-square me-1"></i>Modified (' + d.changed.length + ')</div><ul class="small mb-0">';
                d.changed.forEach(function(c){ h += '<li><code>' + esc(c.control_id) + '</code> — ' + esc(c.fields.join(', ')) + '</li>'; });
                h += '</ul></div>';
              }
              if (d.profileChanges.length) {
                h += '<div class="mb-0"><div class="fw-semibold text-secondary mb-1"><i class="bi bi-sliders me-1"></i>Profile changes</div><ul class="small mb-0">';
                d.profileChanges.forEach(function(p){ h += '<li>' + esc(p.field) + ': <span class="text-muted">' + esc(p.from) + '</span> → <strong>' + esc(p.to) + '</strong></li>'; });
                h += '</ul></div>';
              }
            }
            body.innerHTML = h;
          })
          .catch(function(e){ body.innerHTML = '<div class="alert alert-danger mb-0">' + esc(e.message || 'Could not load summary') + '</div>'; });
      };
    
      window.saaConfirmRevert = function(version, label){
        pendingRevert = version;
        document.getElementById('revertVersionNum').textContent = version;
        document.getElementById('revertVersionLabel').textContent = label ? ('— ' + label) : '';
        var hist = bootstrap.Modal.getInstance(document.getElementById('versionHistoryModal'));
        if (hist) hist.hide();
        new bootstrap.Modal(document.getElementById('revertConfirmModal')).show();
      };
    
      var confirmBtn = document.getElementById('revertConfirmBtn');
      if (confirmBtn) confirmBtn.addEventListener('click', function(){
        if (pendingRevert == null) return;
        var btn = this;
        btn.disabled = true;
        btn.innerHTML = '<span class="spinner-border spinner-border-sm me-1"></span>Reverting…';
        fetch('/admin/assessments/' + assessmentId + '/revert/' + pendingRevert, {
          method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
        }).then(function(r){ return r.json().then(function(d){ return { ok: r.ok, d: d }; }); })
          .then(function(res){
            if (!res.ok || !res.d.success) throw new Error((res.d && res.d.error) || 'Revert failed');
            window.location.reload();
          })
          .catch(function(e){
            alert(e.message || 'Revert failed');
            btn.disabled = false;
            btn.innerHTML = '<i class="bi bi-arrow-counterclockwise me-1"></i>Revert &amp; create new version';
          });
      });
    
      window.saaCreateCheckpoint = function(){
        var label = prompt('Name this checkpoint (optional):', 'Checkpoint');
        if (label === null) return;
        fetch('/admin/assessments/' + assessmentId + '/checkpoint', {
          method: 'POST', headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
          body: JSON.stringify({ label: label || 'Checkpoint' })
        }).then(function(r){ return r.json().then(function(d){ return { ok: r.ok, d: d }; }); })
          .then(function(res){
            if (!res.ok || !res.d.success) throw new Error((res.d && res.d.error) || 'Could not save checkpoint');
            window.location.reload();
          })
          .catch(function(e){ alert(e.message || 'Could not save checkpoint'); });
      };
    })();
    </script>
    
    <style>
      .aia-launch{position:fixed;right:22px;bottom:22px;z-index:1200;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;
        background:linear-gradient(120deg,#6d28d9,#4f46e5);color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:999px;padding:12px 18px;
        font-weight:700;font-size:14px;cursor:pointer;box-shadow:0 12px 30px rgba(79,70,229,.35)}
      .aia-launch:hover{filter:brightness(1.05)}
      .aia-launch .bi{font-size:16px}
      .aia-scrim{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(15,23,42,.45);z-index:1290;opacity:0;visibility:hidden;transition:opacity .2s ease}
      .aia-scrim.open{opacity:1;visibility:visible}
      .aia-panel{position:fixed;top:0;right:0;height:100vh;height:100dvh;width:min(420px,96vw);z-index:1300;background:#fff;box-sizing:border-box;
        border-left:1px solid #e5e7eb;box-shadow:-14px 0 40px rgba(15,23,42,.18);display:flex;flex-direction:column;
        transform:translateX(100%);transition:transform .25s ease}
      .aia-panel.open{transform:none}
      .aia-head{background:linear-gradient(120deg,#4f46e5,#6d28d9);color:#fff;padding:14px 16px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
      .aia-head .ico{width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:9px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.16);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:17px;flex:0 0 auto}
      .aia-head h3{margin:0;font-size:15px;font-weight:750;line-height:1.15;color:#fff !important}
      .aia-msg.bot strong{font-weight:700;color:#111}
      .aia-msg.bot code{font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;background:#eef0fe;color:#3730a3;padding:1px 5px;border-radius:5px}
      .aia-msg.bot ul{margin:6px 0;padding-left:20px}
      .aia-msg.bot li{margin:2px 0}
      .aia-msg.bot > div{margin:0 0 3px}
      .aia-head p{margin:1px 0 0;font-size:11.5px;color:#dcd7ff}
      .aia-head .aia-help{margin-left:auto;flex:0 0 auto;width:24px;height:24px;border-radius:50%;border:1.5px solid rgba(255,255,255,.55);color:#fff;display:grid;place-items:center;font-weight:800;font-size:13px;text-decoration:none;line-height:1}
      .aia-head .aia-help:hover{background:rgba(255,255,255,.2)}
      .aia-head .x{margin-left:8px;background:none;border:none;color:#fff;font-size:20px;cursor:pointer;line-height:1}
      .aia-msgs{flex:1;overflow:auto;padding:14px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:12px;background:#f7f7fb}
      .aia-msg{max-width:88%;padding:10px 13px;border-radius:13px;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.5;white-space:pre-wrap;word-wrap:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
      .aia-msg.user{align-self:flex-end;background:#4f46e5;color:#fff;border-bottom-right-radius:4px}
      .aia-msg.bot{align-self:flex-start;background:#fff;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;color:#22303a;border-bottom-left-radius:4px}
      .aia-msg.sys{align-self:center;background:#eef0fe;color:#4338ca;font-size:12px;text-align:center;border-radius:8px}
      .aia-actions{align-self:flex-start;max-width:96%;background:#fff;border:1px solid #ddd6fe;border-radius:12px;padding:10px 12px;width:100%}
      .aia-actions h4{margin:0 0 8px;font-size:12px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.03em;text-transform:uppercase;color:#6d28d9}
      .aia-act-head{margin-bottom:8px}
      .aia-act-head .btns{display:flex;gap:8px;flex-wrap:wrap}
      .aia-act-head .btns button{font-size:12px;font-weight:700;border-radius:7px;padding:5px 12px;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid transparent}
      .aia-act-head .apall{background:#4f46e5;color:#fff}
      .aia-act-head .dzall{background:#fff;border-color:#d1d5db;color:#374151}
      .aia-act-head .btns button[disabled]{opacity:.5;cursor:not-allowed}
      .aia-applying{margin-top:8px;font-size:12.5px;color:#6d28d9;font-weight:600;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:7px}
      .aia-act{border:1px solid #eee;border-radius:9px;padding:8px 10px;margin-bottom:7px;font-size:12.75px}
      .aia-act .op{font-weight:800;text-transform:uppercase;font-size:10.5px;letter-spacing:.04em;padding:1px 7px;border-radius:6px;margin-right:6px}
      .aia-act .op.remove{background:#fdecec;color:#b91c1c}
      .aia-act .op.add{background:#e7f6ec;color:#15803d}
      .aia-act .op.tailor{background:#eef2ff;color:#4338ca}
      .aia-act .ids{font-family:ui-monospace,Menlo,monospace;font-size:12px;color:#111}
      .aia-act .reason{color:#6b7280;margin-top:3px}
      .aia-act .btns{display:flex;gap:8px;margin-top:8px}
      .aia-act .btns button{font-size:12px;font-weight:700;border-radius:7px;padding:5px 12px;cursor:pointer;border:1px solid transparent}
      .aia-act .ap{background:#4f46e5;color:#fff}
      .aia-act .dz{background:#fff;border-color:#d1d5db;color:#374151}
      .aia-act.done{opacity:.6}
      .aia-act.done .btns{display:none}
      .aia-foot{border-top:1px solid #eee;padding:10px;display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-end;background:#fff}
      .aia-foot textarea{flex:1;resize:none;border:1.5px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;padding:9px 11px;font-size:13.5px;font-family:inherit;max-height:120px}
      .aia-foot textarea:focus{outline:none;border-color:#8b5cf6;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(139,92,246,.15)}
      .aia-foot .send{background:#4f46e5;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:10px;width:42px;height:42px;font-size:18px;cursor:pointer;flex:0 0 auto}
      .aia-foot .send[disabled]{opacity:.5;cursor:not-allowed}
      .aia-typing{align-self:flex-start;color:#8b8b9a;font-size:12.5px;font-style:italic}
      /* Full-panel busy overlay: takes over the chat body while changes are applied. */
      .aia-overlay{position:absolute;left:0;right:0;bottom:0;top:60px;background:rgba(247,247,251,.86);display:none;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:12px;z-index:6;color:#4f46e5;font-weight:650;font-size:13.5px;text-align:center;padding:20px}
      .aia-overlay.on{display:flex}
      .aia-overlay .spinner-border{width:2rem;height:2rem}
      .aia-reload{margin-top:9px;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:7px;background:#4f46e5;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:8px;padding:7px 14px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer}
      .aia-reload:hover{filter:brightness(1.06)}
      .aia-hist-btn{margin-left:8px;flex:0 0 auto;background:rgba(255,255,255,.16);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.4);color:#fff;border-radius:7px;padding:3px 9px;font-size:11.5px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:5px}
      .aia-hist-btn:hover{background:rgba(255,255,255,.28)}
      .aia-hist-btn[disabled]{opacity:.5;cursor:default}
      .aia-sugs{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;margin-top:2px}
      .aia-sugs .lbl{width:100%;font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;color:#8b8b9a;margin-bottom:1px}
      .aia-sugs button{background:#eef0fe;color:#4338ca;border:1px solid #ddd6fe;border-radius:14px;padding:4px 11px;font-size:12px;cursor:pointer;text-align:left}
      .aia-sugs button:hover{background:#e0e3fd}
      .aia-hist-divider{align-self:stretch;text-align:center;color:#9aa;font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;margin:2px 0;border-bottom:1px solid #e5e7eb;padding-bottom:6px}
      @media (prefers-reduced-motion:reduce){.aia-panel{transition:none}}
      /* Phones: the panel takes the full screen so nothing is clipped off the right edge. */
      @media (max-width:640px){
        .aia-panel{width:100vw;max-width:100vw;height:100vh;height:100dvh;border-left:none}
        .aia-launch span{display:none}
        .aia-launch{right:16px;bottom:16px;padding:0;width:52px;height:52px;justify-content:center;border-radius:50%}
        .aia-launch .bi{font-size:20px}
        .aia-msg{max-width:92%}
      }
      body.aia-locked{overflow:hidden}
    </style>
    
    <button type="button" class="aia-launch" id="aiaLaunch" aria-label="Open the Aegis SA Assistant">
      <i class="bi bi-robot" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Aegis SA Assistant</span>
    </button>
    
    <div class="aia-scrim" id="aiaScrim" aria-hidden="true"></div>
    <aside class="aia-panel" id="aiaPanel" role="dialog" aria-modal="false" aria-labelledby="aiaTitle" aria-hidden="true">
      <div class="aia-head">
        <div class="ico" aria-hidden="true"><i class="bi bi-robot"></i></div>
        <div>
          <h3 id="aiaTitle">Aegis SA Assistant (AI)</h3>
          <p id="aiaSub">Ask to refine, add or remove controls</p>
        </div>
        <button type="button" class="aia-hist-btn" id="aiaHistBtn" title="Show this assessment's chat from the last 24 hours">
          <i class="bi bi-clock-history"></i><span>History</span>
        </button>
        <a class="aia-help" href="/assistant-help" target="_blank" rel="noopener" title="How to use the assistant" aria-label="How to use the Aegis SA Assistant (opens in a new tab)">?</a>
        <button type="button" class="x" id="aiaClose" aria-label="Close assistant">&times;</button>
      </div>
      <div class="aia-msgs" id="aiaMsgs"></div>
      <div class="aia-foot">
        <textarea id="aiaInput" rows="1" placeholder="e.g. Remove controls specific to on-premises hosting…"></textarea>
        <button type="button" class="send" id="aiaSend" aria-label="Send" disabled><i class="bi bi-send"></i></button>
      </div>
      <div class="aia-overlay" id="aiaOverlay" aria-hidden="true">
        <span class="spinner-border" role="status" aria-hidden="true"></span>
        <span>Applying changes…</span>
      </div>
    </aside>
    
    <script>
    window.AIAssist = (function(){
      var panel, scrim, msgs, input, sendBtn, launch, closeBtn, histBtn, history = [], booted = false, greeted = false, historyShown = false;
      function cfg(){ return window.AIAssistConfig || {}; }
      function el(){ panel=document.getElementById('aiaPanel');scrim=document.getElementById('aiaScrim');msgs=document.getElementById('aiaMsgs');input=document.getElementById('aiaInput');
        sendBtn=document.getElementById('aiaSend');launch=document.getElementById('aiaLaunch');closeBtn=document.getElementById('aiaClose');histBtn=document.getElementById('aiaHistBtn'); }
      function open(){ panel.classList.add('open');panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
        if(scrim){ scrim.classList.add('open'); scrim.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false'); }
        document.body.classList.add('aia-locked'); input.focus(); greet(); }
      function close(){ panel.classList.remove('open');panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
        if(scrim){ scrim.classList.remove('open'); scrim.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true'); }
        document.body.classList.remove('aia-locked'); }
      function greet(){
        if(greeted) return; greeted = true;
        var c=cfg();
        var g = c.greeting || 'Hi — I’m your Aegis SA Assistant. Ask me to remove, add or tailor controls, or ask whether the current set covers a particular scope.';
        add('bot', g);
        renderSuggestions();
      }
      // Context-aware starter prompts for the record in view. Clicking one sends it.
      function renderSuggestions(){
        var c=cfg();
        var list=(c.suggestions||[]).filter(Boolean);
        if(!list.length) return;
        var wrap=document.createElement('div'); wrap.className='aia-sugs';
        var lbl=document.createElement('span'); lbl.className='lbl'; lbl.textContent=c.suggestionsLabel||'Try asking';
        wrap.appendChild(lbl);
        list.forEach(function(text){
          var b=document.createElement('button'); b.type='button'; b.textContent=text;
          b.onclick=function(){ input.value=text; sendBtn.disabled=false; wrap.remove(); send(); };
          wrap.appendChild(b);
        });
        msgs.appendChild(wrap); scrollBottom();
      }
      // Show the last 24h of persisted chat for this assessment/project, inserted above
      // the current session so the fresh greeting stays at the bottom.
      async function loadHistory(){
        if(historyShown) return;
        var c=cfg();
        var qs = c.assessmentId ? ('assessmentId='+encodeURIComponent(c.assessmentId))
               : (c.projectId ? ('projectId='+encodeURIComponent(c.projectId)) : '');
        if(!qs) return;
        if(histBtn) histBtn.disabled=true;
        try{
          var r=await fetch('/api/ai/assessment-chat/history?'+qs);
          var data=await r.json();
          var items=(data && data.history) || [];
          historyShown=true;
          if(!items.length){ add('sys','No chat history in the last 24 hours.'); if(histBtn){ histBtn.disabled=true; histBtn.title='No history in the last 24 hours'; } scrollBottom(); return; }
          var frag=document.createDocumentFragment();
          var div=document.createElement('div'); div.className='aia-hist-divider'; div.textContent='Earlier — last 24 hours'; frag.appendChild(div);
          items.forEach(function(row){
            var u=document.createElement('div'); u.className='aia-msg user'; u.textContent=row.message||''; frag.appendChild(u);
            if(row.ai_reply){ var b=document.createElement('div'); b.className='aia-msg bot'; b.innerHTML=mdToHtml(cleanReply(row.ai_reply)); frag.appendChild(b); }
          });
          msgs.insertBefore(frag, msgs.firstChild);
          if(histBtn){ histBtn.disabled=true; histBtn.title='History shown'; }
        }catch(e){ add('sys','Could not load chat history.'); if(histBtn) histBtn.disabled=false; }
      }
      function cleanReply(t){
        // Belt-and-suspenders: if a reply still arrives as raw JSON, extract .reply.
        var s=String(t||'').trim();
        if(s.charAt(0)==='{' && s.indexOf('"reply"')>-1){
          try{ var o=JSON.parse(s); if(o&&o.reply) return String(o.reply); }catch(e){
            var m=s.match(/"reply"\s*:\s*"((?:[^"\\]|\\.)*)"/); if(m) return m[1].replace(/\\n/g,'\n').replace(/\\"/g,'"').replace(/\\\\/g,'\\');
          }
        }
        return s;
      }
      function mdToHtml(t){
        var lines=String(t).split('\n'), html='', inList=false;
        function esc(s){ return escapeHtml(s).replace(/`([^`]+)`/g,'<code>$1</code>').replace(/\*\*([^*]+)\*\*/g,'<strong>$1</strong>'); }
        lines.forEach(function(ln){
          var m=ln.match(/^\s*[-*]\s+(.*)$/);
          if(m){ if(!inList){html+='<ul>';inList=true;} html+='<li>'+esc(m[1])+'</li>'; }
          else { if(inList){html+='</ul>';inList=false;} if(ln.trim()==='') html+='<br>'; else html+='<div>'+esc(ln)+'</div>'; }
        });
        if(inList) html+='</ul>';
        return html;
      }
      function add(kind, text){
        var d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='aia-msg '+kind;
        if(kind==='bot'){ d.innerHTML=mdToHtml(cleanReply(text)); } else { d.textContent=text; }
        msgs.appendChild(d); msgs.scrollTop=msgs.scrollHeight; return d;
      }
      function typing(on){
        var t=document.getElementById('aiaTyping');
        if(on){ if(!t){ t=document.createElement('div'); t.id='aiaTyping'; t.className='aia-typing'; t.textContent='Assistant is thinking…'; msgs.appendChild(t);} }
        else if(t){ t.remove(); }
        msgs.scrollTop=msgs.scrollHeight;
      }
      function renderActions(actions){
        if(!actions || !actions.length) return;
        var valid=actions.filter(function(a){ return ['remove','add','tailor'].indexOf((a.op||'').toLowerCase())>-1; });
        if(!valid.length) return;
        var wrap=document.createElement('div'); wrap.className='aia-actions';
        var head=document.createElement('div'); head.className='aia-act-head';
        head.innerHTML='<h4>Proposed changes — approve to apply</h4>'+
          '<div class="btns"><button type="button" class="apall">Approve all ('+valid.length+')</button><button type="button" class="dzall">Dismiss all</button></div>';
        wrap.appendChild(head);
        var rows=[];
        valid.forEach(function(a){
          var op=(a.op||'').toLowerCase(); var ids=(a.controlIds||[]).join(', ');
          var row=document.createElement('div'); row.className='aia-act'; row._action=a; row._done=false;
          row.innerHTML='<div><span class="op '+op+'">'+op+'</span><span class="ids">'+(ids||'')+'</span></div>'+
            (a.reason?'<div class="reason">'+escapeHtml(a.reason)+'</div>':'')+
            (op==='tailor'&&a.tailoring?'<div class="reason"><em>'+escapeHtml(a.tailoring)+'</em></div>':'')+
            '<div class="btns"><button type="button" class="ap">Approve</button><button type="button" class="dz">Dismiss</button></div>';
          row.querySelector('.ap').onclick=function(){ applyItems([{a:a,row:row}], wrap); };
          row.querySelector('.dz').onclick=function(){ row._done=true; row.classList.add('done'); };
          rows.push(row); wrap.appendChild(row);
        });
        head.querySelector('.apall').onclick=function(){
          var pending=rows.filter(function(r){ return !r._done; }).map(function(r){ return {a:r._action,row:r}; });
          if(pending.length) applyItems(pending, wrap);
        };
        head.querySelector('.dzall').onclick=function(){ rows.forEach(function(r){ if(!r._done){ r._done=true; r.classList.add('done'); } }); };
        msgs.appendChild(wrap); msgs.scrollTop=msgs.scrollHeight;
      }
      function scrollBottom(){ if(msgs) msgs.scrollTop=msgs.scrollHeight; }
      // Full-panel busy state: the overlay takes over the chat body so the user
      // cannot interact with the conversation while changes are being applied.
      function panelBusy(on){
        var ov=document.getElementById('aiaOverlay');
        if(ov){ ov.classList.toggle('on', !!on); ov.setAttribute('aria-hidden', on?'false':'true'); }
        if(input){ input.disabled=!!on; }
        if(sendBtn){ sendBtn.disabled = !!on || !(input && input.value.trim()); }
      }
      // System confirmation that carries a real Reload button (bootstrap-icons).
      function addSysReload(text){
        var d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='aia-msg sys';
        d.innerHTML=escapeHtml(text)+'<div><button type="button" class="aia-reload"><i class="bi bi-arrow-clockwise"></i> Reload page</button></div>';
        d.querySelector('.aia-reload').onclick=function(){ window.location.reload(); };
        msgs.appendChild(d); scrollBottom(); return d;
      }
      async function applyItems(items, wrap){
        var c=cfg(); var actions=items.map(function(it){ return it.a; });
        panelBusy(true);
        try{
          if(c.applyEndpoint){
            // Persisted apply (e.g. reviewing an existing assessment).
            var r=await fetch(c.applyEndpoint,{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({actions:actions,assessmentId:c.assessmentId||null})});
            var data=await r.json();
            if(!r.ok||!data.success) throw new Error(data.error||'Could not apply the changes.');
            items.forEach(function(it){ it.row._done=true; it.row.classList.add('done'); });
            panelBusy(false);
            addSysReload('✓ '+(data.applied!=null? data.applied+' change(s) saved.' : 'Changes saved.')+' Reload the page to see the updated controls.');
          } else {
            // Client-side apply against the on-page form (e.g. creating an assessment).
            try{ (c.applyActions||function(){})(actions); }catch(e){ console.error(e); }
            items.forEach(function(it){ it.row._done=true; it.row.classList.add('done'); });
            panelBusy(false);
            add('sys','✓ Applied '+actions.length+' change(s) to your selection below.');
          }
        }catch(err){ panelBusy(false); add('sys', (err&&err.message)||'Something went wrong applying the changes.'); }
        scrollBottom();
      }
      function escapeHtml(s){ return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(m){return({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[m]);}); }
      async function send(){
        var text=(input.value||'').trim(); if(!text) return;
        add('user', text); history.push({role:'user',content:text}); input.value=''; input.style.height='auto'; sendBtn.disabled=true; typing(true);
        var c=cfg();
        var body={ mode:c.mode||'create', projectId:c.projectId||null, assessmentId:c.assessmentId||null,
          frameworkLabel:c.frameworkLabel||'ITSG-33', profileSummary:c.profileSummary||'', projectContext:c.projectContext||'',
          baselineCount:c.baselineCount||0, message:text, history:history.slice(-8),
          selected:(c.getSelected?c.getSelected():[]).slice(0,400) };
        try{
          var r=await fetch((c.chatEndpoint)||'/api/ai/assessment-chat',{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify(body)});
          var data=await r.json(); typing(false);
          if(!r.ok||!data.success) throw new Error(data.error||'The assistant could not respond.');
          add('bot', data.reply||'(no reply)'); history.push({role:'assistant',content:data.reply||''});
          renderActions(data.actions);
        }catch(e){ typing(false); add('sys', e.message||'Something went wrong.'); }
        sendBtn.disabled=false; input.focus();
      }
      function boot(){
        if(booted) return; booted=true; el();
        if(cfg().subtitle){ document.getElementById('aiaSub').textContent=cfg().subtitle; }
        launch.addEventListener('click', open); closeBtn.addEventListener('click', close);
        if(scrim) scrim.addEventListener('click', close);
        // History button is only useful when there is an assessment/project context.
        if(histBtn){
          if(cfg().assessmentId || cfg().projectId){ histBtn.addEventListener('click', loadHistory); }
          else { histBtn.style.display='none'; }
        }
        input.addEventListener('input', function(){ sendBtn.disabled=!input.value.trim(); input.style.height='auto'; input.style.height=Math.min(120,input.scrollHeight)+'px'; });
        input.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){ e.preventDefault(); if(input.value.trim()) send(); } });
        sendBtn.addEventListener('click', send);
        document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e){ if(e.key==='Escape'&&panel.classList.contains('open')) close(); });
      }
      document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', boot);
      return { open:open, close:close, boot:boot };
    })();
    </script><script>
    // Aegis SA Assistant — review mode (assessor Q&A + guidance on this assessment).
    window.AIAssistConfig = {
      mode: 'review',
      assessmentId: 2,
      projectId: 5,
      frameworkLabel: 'ITSG-33',
      profileSummary: 'profile PBMM; C/I/A protected-b / medium / medium',
      subtitle: 'Review help · ask about this assessment',
      greeting: 'Hi — ask me anything about this assessment: whether the controls cover a given scope, what a control expects, or how to word an audit note. If I propose control changes, approving them saves to this assessment.',
      // Approving proposed changes here PERSISTS them to the assessment (add / remove / tailor),
      // then prompts a refresh to see the updated control list.
      suggestionsLabel: &quot;Try asking&quot;,
      suggestions: [&quot;Do these controls cover our hosting model?&quot;, &quot;Which controls still lack evidence?&quot;, &quot;Help me word an audit note&quot;],
      applyEndpoint: '/admin/assessments/2/apply-ai-actions',
      getSelected: function(){
        return Array.prototype.map.call(document.querySelectorAll('.control-id'), function(el){
          var p = el.closest('.control-card') || el.parentElement;
          var t = p ? p.querySelector('.control-title') : null;
          return { id: (el.textContent||'').trim(), title: t ? (t.textContent||'').trim() : '' };
        }).filter(function(c){ return c.id; });
      }
    };
    </script>
    
      <button type="button" class="cb-launch" id="cbLaunch" aria-label="Discussion">
        <i class="bi bi-chat-dots" aria-hidden="true"></i><span>Collaboration</span>
        <span class="cb-badge" id="cbBadge" hidden>0</span>
      </button>
      
      <div class="cb-scrim" id="cbScrim" aria-hidden="true"></div>
      <aside class="cb-panel" id="cbPanel" role="dialog" aria-labelledby="cbTitle" aria-hidden="true">
        <div class="cb-head">
          <div class="ico" aria-hidden="true"><i class="bi bi-chat-dots"></i></div>
          <div class="flex-grow-1">
            <h3 id="cbTitle">Collaboration</h3>
            <p id="cbSub">Discussion</p>
          </div>
          <button type="button" class="x" id="cbClose" aria-label="Close">&times;</button>
        </div>
        <div class="cb-msgs" id="cbMsgs"></div>
        <div class="cb-foot">
          <div class="cb-mentions" id="cbMentions" hidden></div>
          <div class="cb-compose">
            <textarea id="cbInput" rows="1" placeholder="Write a message… use @ to mention a person or record"></textarea>
            <button type="button" class="send" id="cbSend" aria-label="Send" disabled><i class="bi bi-send"></i></button>
          </div>
          <p class="cb-privacy"><i class="bi bi-shield-lock me-1"></i>Discussion is never shared with the AI assistant.</p>
        </div>
      </aside>
      
      <style>
        .cb-launch{position:fixed;right:22px;bottom:86px;z-index:1200;display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;
          background:#0f766e;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:999px;padding:11px 18px;font-weight:700;font-size:.86rem;
          box-shadow:0 6px 20px rgba(15,118,110,.35);cursor:pointer}
        .cb-launch:hover{filter:brightness(1.06)}
        .cb-badge{background:#fff;color:#0f766e;border-radius:999px;padding:1px 7px;font-size:.72rem;font-weight:800}
        .cb-scrim{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(15,23,42,.45);z-index:1290;opacity:0;visibility:hidden;transition:opacity .2s}
        .cb-scrim.open{opacity:1;visibility:visible}
        .cb-panel{position:fixed;top:0;right:0;height:100vh;height:100dvh;width:min(420px,96vw);z-index:1300;background:#fff;
          box-sizing:border-box;border-left:1px solid #e5e7eb;box-shadow:-14px 0 40px rgba(15,23,42,.18);
          display:flex;flex-direction:column;transform:translateX(102%);transition:transform .22s ease}
        .cb-panel.open{transform:none}
        .cb-head{background:linear-gradient(120deg,#0f766e,#115e59);color:#fff;padding:14px 16px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px}
        .cb-head .ico{width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:9px;background:rgba(255,255,255,.16);display:grid;place-items:center;font-size:17px;flex:0 0 auto}
        .cb-head h3{margin:0;font-size:15px;font-weight:750;color:#fff !important}
        .cb-head p{margin:1px 0 0;font-size:11.5px;color:#d7f0ec}
        .cb-head .x{background:none;border:none;color:#fff;font-size:20px;cursor:pointer;line-height:1}
        .cb-msgs{flex:1;overflow:auto;padding:14px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:10px;background:#f7f9f9}
        .cb-msg{background:#fff;border:1px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:11px;padding:9px 12px;font-size:13.5px;line-height:1.45;
          word-wrap:break-word;overflow-wrap:anywhere}
        .cb-msg .who{font-weight:700;font-size:12px;color:#0f766e}
        .cb-msg .when{font-size:11px;color:#8b95a1;margin-left:6px}
        .cb-msg .where{font-size:11px;color:#8b95a1}
        .cb-msg .mention{background:#e0f2f1;color:#0f766e;border-radius:5px;padding:0 4px;font-weight:600}
        .cb-empty{color:#8b95a1;font-size:13px;text-align:center;padding:24px 8px}
        .cb-foot{border-top:1px solid #eee;padding:10px;background:#fff}
        .cb-compose{display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:flex-end}
        .cb-compose textarea{flex:1;resize:none;border:1.5px solid #e5e7eb;border-radius:10px;padding:9px 11px;font-size:13.5px;font-family:inherit;max-height:120px}
        .cb-compose textarea:focus{outline:none;border-color:#14b8a6;box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(20,184,166,.15)}
        .cb-compose .send{background:#0f766e;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:10px;width:42px;height:42px;font-size:18px;cursor:pointer;flex:0 0 auto}
        .cb-compose .send[disabled]{opacity:.5;cursor:not-allowed}
        .cb-privacy{margin:7px 0 0;font-size:11px;color:#7b8794}
        .cb-mentions{display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:5px;margin-bottom:7px}
        .cb-mentions button{background:#e0f2f1;color:#0f766e;border:1px solid #b2dfdb;border-radius:6px;padding:2px 8px;font-size:11.5px;cursor:pointer}
        @media (max-width:640px){
          .cb-panel{width:100vw;max-width:100vw;border-left:none}
          .cb-launch{right:16px;bottom:78px;padding:0;width:48px;height:48px;justify-content:center;border-radius:50%}
          .cb-launch span:not(.cb-badge){display:none}
        }
        body.cb-locked{overflow:hidden}
      </style>
      
      <script>
      (function(){
        function cfg(){ return window.CollabConfig || {}; }
        var panel, scrim, msgs, input, sendBtn, launch, closeBtn, badge, mentionBar, loaded=false, people=[], records=[];
        function esc(s){ return String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,function(m){return({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'})[m];}); }
        function el(){ panel=document.getElementById('cbPanel');scrim=document.getElementById('cbScrim');msgs=document.getElementById('cbMsgs');
          input=document.getElementById('cbInput');sendBtn=document.getElementById('cbSend');launch=document.getElementById('cbLaunch');
          closeBtn=document.getElementById('cbClose');badge=document.getElementById('cbBadge');mentionBar=document.getElementById('cbMentions'); }
        function open(){ panel.classList.add('open');panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden','false');
          scrim.classList.add('open');document.body.classList.add('cb-locked');
          if(!loaded) load(); input.focus(); }
        function close(){ panel.classList.remove('open');panel.setAttribute('aria-hidden','true');
          scrim.classList.remove('open');document.body.classList.remove('cb-locked'); }
        function base(){ return '/admin/projects/'+cfg().projectId+'/collab'; }
        function render(list){
          msgs.innerHTML='';
          if(!list.length){ var e=document.createElement('div'); e.className='cb-empty'; e.textContent=cfg().emptyText||'No messages yet.'; msgs.appendChild(e); return; }
          list.forEach(function(m){
            var d=document.createElement('div'); d.className='cb-msg';
            var body=esc(m.body).replace(/@([\w.@+-]+)/g,'<span class="mention">@$1</span>');
            var where = (m.entity_type && m.entity_type!=='project') ? ' <span class="where">· '+esc(m.entity_type.replace('_',' '))+(m.entity_id?(' #'+m.entity_id):'')+'</span>' : '';
            d.innerHTML='<div><span class="who">'+esc(m.user_name)+'</span><span class="when">'+esc((m.created_at||'').replace('T',' ').slice(0,16))+'</span>'+where+'</div><div>'+body+'</div>';
            msgs.appendChild(d);
          });
          msgs.scrollTop=msgs.scrollHeight;
        }
        function renderMentions(){
          if(!mentionBar) return;
          var items=[].concat(people.slice(0,6).map(function(p){return {t:'@'+(p.email? p.email.split('@')[0] : p.name), title:p.name};}),
                              records.slice(0,4).map(function(r){return {t:'@'+r.label, title:r.label};}));
          if(!items.length){ mentionBar.hidden=true; return; }
          mentionBar.hidden=false; mentionBar.innerHTML='';
          items.forEach(function(it){ var b=document.createElement('button'); b.type='button'; b.textContent=it.t; b.title=it.title;
            b.onclick=function(){ input.value=(input.value?input.value+' ':'')+it.t+' '; input.focus(); sendBtn.disabled=!input.value.trim(); };
            mentionBar.appendChild(b); });
        }
        function load(){
          var c=cfg(); var q=[];
          if(c.entityType) q.push('entityType='+encodeURIComponent(c.entityType));
          if(c.entityId) q.push('entityId='+encodeURIComponent(c.entityId));
          fetch(base()+(q.length?('?'+q.join('&')):''))
            .then(function(r){return r.json();})
            .then(function(d){ if(!d.success) throw new Error(d.error||'error');
              loaded=true; people=d.members||[]; records=d.records||[]; render(d.messages||[]); renderMentions();
              if(badge){ badge.hidden=!(d.messages||[]).length; badge.textContent=(d.messages||[]).length; } })
            .catch(function(){ msgs.innerHTML='<div class="cb-empty">—</div>'; });
        }
        function send(){
          var text=(input.value||'').trim(); if(!text) return;
          var c=cfg(); sendBtn.disabled=true;
          fetch(base(),{method:'POST',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},
            body:JSON.stringify({body:text,entityType:c.entityType||'project',entityId:c.entityId||null})})
            .then(function(r){return r.json();})
            .then(function(d){ if(!d.success) throw new Error(d.error||'error');
              input.value=''; input.style.height='auto'; load(); })
            .catch(function(){ sendBtn.disabled=false; });
        }
        function boot(){
          if(!document.getElementById('cbPanel')) return;
          el();
          if(!cfg().projectId){ launch.style.display='none'; return; }
          launch.addEventListener('click',open); closeBtn.addEventListener('click',close); scrim.addEventListener('click',close);
          input.addEventListener('input',function(){ sendBtn.disabled=!input.value.trim();
            input.style.height='auto'; input.style.height=Math.min(120,input.scrollHeight)+'px'; });
          input.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){ if(e.key==='Enter'&&!e.shiftKey){ e.preventDefault(); if(input.value.trim()) send(); } });
          sendBtn.addEventListener('click',send);
          document.addEventListener('keydown',function(e){ if(e.key==='Escape'&&panel.classList.contains('open')) close(); });
          load();
        }
        document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',boot);
        window.Collab={open:open,close:close,reload:load};
      })();
      </script>  <script>window.CollabConfig = { projectId: 5, entityType: 'assessment', entityId: 2 };</script>
      </main>
    
      <footer id="gc-footer" role="contentinfo">
        <div class="gc-footer-main">
          <div class="container gc-edge">
            <nav aria-label="About this site">
              <h2 class="visually-hidden">About this site</h2>
              <ul class="gc-footer-links">
                <li><a href="/help">Help</a></li>
                <li><a href="/pricing">Pricing</a></li>
                <li><a href="/self-assessment">Security Self-Assessment</a></li>
                <li><a href="/admin/login">Sign In</a></li>
              </ul>
            </nav>
            <p style="font-size:.8125rem;margin:0;opacity:.7;">&copy; 2026 Vanguard Cloud Services — Security Assessment &amp; Authorization Platform</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="gc-footer-sub">
          <div class="container gc-edge gc-footer-sub-inner">
            <div class="gc-modified">Date modified: <time>2026-08-22</time></div>
            <span style="font-weight:600;color:var(--cs-nav);font-size:.875rem;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.35rem;">
              <img src="/images/logo-large@2xVCS.png" alt="VCS" style="height:28px;">
              Vanguard Cloud Services
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </footer>
    
      <script src="https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/bootstrap@5.3.2/dist/js/bootstrap.bundle.min.js"></script>
      
    </body>
